<ul><li><b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Kohat_attack" title="2026 Kohat attack">An attack on a police complex</a></b> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kohat" title="Kohat">Kohat</a>, Pakistan, leaves at least 31 people dead.</li>
<li>The <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tilcayo" title="Tilcayo">tilcayo</a></b> <i>(pictured)</i>, a new species of tiger cat, is formally identified.</li>
<li>Former leader of Kosovo <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hashim_Thaçi" title="Hashim Thaçi">Hashim Thaçi</a></b> is sentenced to 25 years in prison for <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/War_crimes_in_the_Kosovo_War#KLA_war_crimes" title="War crimes in the Kosovo War">war crimes during his leadership</a></b> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kosovo_Liberation_Army" title="Kosovo Liberation Army">Kosovo Liberation Army</a>.</li>
<li>At <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/78th_Primetime_Emmy_Awards" title="78th Primetime Emmy Awards">the Primetime Emmy Awards</a></b>, <i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Widow's_Bay" title="Widow's Bay">Widow's Bay</a></i> wins <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Primetime_Emmy_Award_for_Outstanding_Comedy_Series" title="Primetime Emmy Award for Outstanding Comedy Series">Outstanding Comedy Series</a>, and <i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Pitt" title="The Pitt">The Pitt</a></i> wins <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Primetime_Emmy_Award_for_Outstanding_Drama_Series" title="Primetime Emmy Award for Outstanding Drama Series">Outstanding Drama Series</a>.</li>
<li>In Turkey, hundreds of people are arrested in <b><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Turkish_LGBTQ_raids" title="2026 Turkish LGBTQ raids">a crackdown on LGBTQ expression</a></b> and subsequent protests.</li></ul>

<p><b>Law and crime</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Crime_in_South_Africa" title="Crime in South Africa">Crime in South Africa</a>
<ul><li>Ten people are killed and 11 others are injured in a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_mass_shootings_in_South_Africa" title="List of mass shootings in South Africa">mass shooting</a> at a store in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lwandle" title="Lwandle">Lwandle</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/City_of_Cape_Town" title="City of Cape Town">City of Cape Town</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_Africa" title="South Africa">South Africa</a>. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/South_African_Police_Service" title="South African Police Service">Police</a> search for the perpetrators. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.euronews.com/2026/09/27/two-mass-shootings-in-south-africa-leave-at-least-27-dead" class="external text">(Euronews)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

<p><b>Armed conflicts and attacks</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Insurgency_in_Khyber_Pakhtunkhwa" title="Insurgency in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa">Insurgency in Khyber Pakhtunkhwa</a>
<ul><li>A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pakistani_Taliban" title="Pakistani Taliban">Pakistani Taliban</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_attack" title="Suicide attack">suicide bomber</a> blows himself up at a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khyber_Pakhtunkhwa_Police" title="Khyber Pakhtunkhwa Police">police</a> checkpoint in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dera_Ismail_Khan" title="Dera Ismail Khan">Dera Ismail Khan</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khyber_Pakhtunkhwa" title="Khyber Pakhtunkhwa">Khyber Pakhtunkhwa</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pakistan" title="Pakistan">Pakistan</a>, killing 12 people and wounding 30 more. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.aljazeera.com/amp/news/2026/9/26/blast-in-pakistans-dera-ismail-khan-kills-several-people" class="external text">(Al Jazeera)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://apnews.com/article/pakistan-attack-suicide-36b11b45aef7ac67bb1d34cc6e9ff02e" class="external text">(AP)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

<p><b>Disasters and accidents</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Bangkok_floods" title="2026 Bangkok floods">2026 Bangkok floods</a>
<ul><li>In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thailand" title="Thailand">Thailand</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Governor_of_Bangkok" title="Governor of Bangkok">Bangkok governor</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chadchart_Sittipunt" title="Chadchart Sittipunt">Chadchart Sittipunt</a> declares a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Floods_in_Thailand" title="Floods in Thailand">flood disaster</a> across <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bangkok" title="Bangkok">the city</a> as severe flooding kills at least two people and affects more than 84,000 nationwide. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.france24.com/en/live-news/20260926-bangkok-declares-flood-disaster-across-city" class="external text">(AFP via France 24)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://apnews.com/article/thailand-bangkok-flooding-water-heavy-rain-d201411a72a08aa8b4b39f7aa258a212" class="external text">(AP)</a></li></ul></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/September_2026_nor'easter" title="September 2026 nor'easter">September 2026 nor'easter</a>
<ul><li>A <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nor'easter" title="Nor'easter">nor'easter</a> is reported along the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/East_Coast_of_the_United_States" title="East Coast of the United States">East Coast</a> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States" title="United States">United States</a>, forcing the cancellation of 400 flights and leaving more than 68,000 homes without power. One person is killed in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_York_City" title="New York City">New York City</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.reuters.com/business/environment/noreaster-leaves-over-100000-customers-without-power-us-northeast-over-400-2026-09-26/" class="external text">(Reuters)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://abc7ny.com/post/brooklyn-falling-tree-kills-56-year-old-man-east-new-york-parking-lot-police-sources-say/19877365/" class="external text">(WABC-TV)</a></li></ul></li>

<ul><li>Days before the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Brazilian_general_election" title="2026 Brazilian general election">general election</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazilian_president" title="Brazilian president" class="mw-redirect">Brazilian president</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luiz_Inácio_Lula_da_Silva" title="Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva">Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva</a> issues a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Provisional_measure" title="Provisional measure">provisional measure</a> banning <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Online_betting" title="Online betting" class="mw-redirect">online betting</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazil" title="Brazil">Brazil</a>, immediately halting new deposits. Users may withdraw remaining funds until October 5, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazilian_National_Congress" title="Brazilian National Congress">Congress</a> must approve the measure within 120 days for it to remain in force. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.france24.com/en/live-news/20260925-lula-bans-sports-betting-days-ahead-of-brazil-s-election" class="external text">(AFP via France 24)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c914ddj21rzlo" class="external text">(BBC News)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Venezuela" title="Venezuela">Venezuela</a> releases at least 29 detainees whom <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_rights_in_Venezuela" title="Human rights in Venezuela">rights groups</a> classify as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Political_prisoners_in_Venezuela" title="Political prisoners in Venezuela">political prisoners</a> following a second round of talks between <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Venezuela" title="Government of Venezuela">the interim government</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Venezuelan_opposition" title="Venezuelan opposition">opposition</a>, while organizations continue verifying the number and identities of those freed. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.rfi.fr/en/international-news/20260926-venezuela-releases-more-political-prisoners-after-opposition-talks" class="external text">(AFP via RFI)</a></li></ul></li>

Le Priol and Bouvier had links to the dissolved <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Far-right_politics_in_France" title="Far-right politics in France">far-right</a> group <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Groupe_Union_Défense" title="Groupe Union Défense">Groupe Union Défense</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://apnews.com/article/aramburu-rugby-killing-paris-796a8a4b3e5032340f36807ef27e3a12" class="external text">(AP)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c639mm1mrv94o" class="external text">(BBC News)</a></li>

<ul><li>In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Competitive_swimming" title="Competitive swimming">swimming</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Japan" title="Japan">Japan</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shin_Ohashi" title="Shin Ohashi">Shin Ohashi</a> wins gold in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swimming_at_the_2026_Asian_Games_–_Men's_200_metre_breaststroke" title="Swimming at the 2026 Asian Games – Men's 200 metre breaststroke">men's 200-metre breaststroke</a> at the 2026 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asian_Games" title="Asian Games">Asian Games</a> in 2:04.83, breaking <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/China" title="China">China</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qin_Haiyang" title="Qin Haiyang">Qin Haiyang</a>'s 2023 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/200_metres_breaststroke" title="200 metres breaststroke">world record</a> of 2:05.48 by 0.65 seconds. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.afp.com/en/japan-swimmer-ohashi-17-shrugs-covid-smash-world-record" class="external text">(AFP)</a></li></ul></li></ul></li>

<ul><li>In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Baseball" title="Baseball">baseball</a>, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Major_League_Baseball" title="Major League Baseball">Major League Baseball</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chicago_White_Sox" title="Chicago White Sox">Chicago White Sox</a> clinches the upcoming <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Major_League_Baseball_postseason" title="2026 Major League Baseball postseason">playoffs</a> for the first time since <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Chicago_White_Sox_season" title="2021 Chicago White Sox season">2021</a>, following three-straight <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_worst_Major_League_Baseball_season_win–loss_records" title="List of worst Major League Baseball season win–loss records">100-loss seasons</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://apnews.com/article/white-sox-royals-score-clinch-25f0e5288dd7165e3a8f94f1185433fd#" class="external text">(AP)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_United_States_naval_blockade_of_Iran" title="2026 United States naval blockade of Iran">2026 United States naval blockade of Iran</a>
<ul><li>The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Central_Command" title="United States Central Command">United States Central Command</a> says that the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Armed_Forces" title="United States Armed Forces">U.S. military</a> has redirected 115 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Commercial_vessel" title="Commercial vessel" class="mw-redirect">commercial vessels</a> since the reinstatement of its <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blockade" title="Blockade">blockade</a> on July 14. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://gulfnews.com/world/mena/115-iran-linked-ships-redirected-us-naval-blockade-on-iran-continues-1.500685751" class="external text">(<i>Gulf News</i>)</a></li></ul></li>
<li>An Indian <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wiper_(occupation)" title="Wiper (occupation)">wiper</a> is killed during an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_ships_attacked_during_the_2026_Iran_war" title="List of ships attacked during the 2026 Iran war">attack</a> on the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bulk_carrier" title="Bulk carrier">bulk carrier</a> MV <i>Cape Dao</i> near the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Strait_of_Hormuz" title="Strait of Hormuz">Strait of Hormuz</a>, with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oman" title="Oman">Oman</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Navy_of_Oman" title="Royal Navy of Oman">navy</a> confirming that 27 crew members have been evacuated. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.arabnews.com/middle-east/one-indian-killed-in-attack-on-ship-heading-toward-india-says-embassy-in-oman-says-3002803" class="external text">(AFP and Reuters via <i>Arab News</i>)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://indianexpress.com/article/world/indian-national-killed-mv-cape-dao-attack-gulf-of-oman-10891112/" class="external text">(<i>The Indian Express</i>)</a></li></ul></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran" title="Iran">Iran</a> reopens <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Education_in_Iran" title="Education in Iran">schools</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Higher_education_in_Iran" title="Higher education in Iran">universities</a> after months of closures caused by the war. About 16.5 million schoolchildren begin the new academic year, with reopening ceremonies held across the country. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.channelstv.com/2026/09/23/iranian-children-back-to-school-after-war-closures/" class="external text">(AFP via Channels Television)</a></li>

<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Colombian_conflict" title="Colombian conflict">Colombian conflict</a>

The group orders the shutdown after <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_Forces_of_Colombia" title="Military Forces of Colombia">security forces</a> kill 13 ACSN members. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.france24.com/en/live-news/20260924-colombians-in-santa-marta-stay-inside-after-violence-wracks-the-city" class="external text">(AFP via France 24)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.reuters.com/world/americas/threats-armed-group-bring-colombias-santa-marta-standstill-2026-09-24/" class="external text">(Reuters)</a></li></ul></li>

Six suspects are arrested following the attack. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://dailypost.ng/2026/09/22/ethnic-clash-kills-15-in-kebbi-as-residents-flee-homes/" class="external text">(<i>Daily Post</i>)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

The group says more than 6,600 women and 640 children are among over 30,000 political prisoners detained since 2021. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.reuters.com/world/asia-pacific/myanmar-female-prisoners-face-torture-neglect-rights-group-says-2026-09-23/" class="external text">(Reuters)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

<p><b>Arts and culture</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Silver" title="Silver">Silver</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_currency" title="Roman currency">Roman coins</a> dating back to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2nd_century" title="2nd century">2nd century</a> are discovered by an amateur archaeologist in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Germany" title="Germany">Germany</a>. The coins are placed on display at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rheinisches_Landesmuseum_Bonn" title="Rheinisches Landesmuseum Bonn">Rheinisches Landesmuseum Bonn</a> museum in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bonn" title="Bonn">Bonn</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/sep/22/roman-coins-germany-amateur-archaeologist-silver-denarii" class="external text">(<i>The Guardian</i>)</a></li></ul>

<p><b>Health and environment</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Atlantic_hurricane_season" title="2026 Atlantic hurricane season">2026 Atlantic hurricane season</a>
<ul><li>The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atlantic_hurricane_season" title="Atlantic hurricane season">Atlantic hurricane season</a> breaks a 112-year <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Atlantic_hurricane_records" title="List of Atlantic hurricane records">record</a> without a hurricane, due to a strong <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026–2027_El_Niño_event" title="2026–2027 El Niño event">El Niño event</a> in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pacific_Ocean" title="Pacific Ocean">Pacific</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.accuweather.com/en/hurricane/atlantic-goes-without-a-hurricane-through-sept-21-breaking-112-year-record/1932278" class="external text">(AccuWeather)</a></li></ul></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tunisia" title="Tunisia">Tunisia</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_meteorological_service" title="National meteorological service">meteorological service</a> reports that summer 2026 is <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Climate_of_Tunisia" title="Climate of Tunisia">the country's hottest</a> since records began in 1950, with average temperatures 2.9<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>°C (5.2<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>°F) above the previous record and repeated <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heat_wave" title="Heat wave">heat waves</a> contributing to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Water_supply_and_sanitation_in_Tunisia" title="Water supply and sanitation in Tunisia">water shortages</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Energy_in_Tunisia" title="Energy in Tunisia">power cuts</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.afp.com/en/tunisia-says-2026-summer-hottest-record" class="external text">(AFP)</a></li></ul>

The FBI investigates the claim. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.reuters.com/world/shinyhunters-hackers-say-they-breached-federal-bureau-investigation-no-immediate-2026-09-22/" class="external text">(Reuters)</a></li>

Kennedy International Airport">JFK</a>, due to technological issues after a construction crew accidentally severed numerous cables, including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Optical_fiber" title="Optical fiber">fiber-optic</a> ones used for communication with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Air_traffic_control" title="Air traffic control">air traffic control</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ckvgyzzgvv4wo" class="external text">(BBC News)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.cnn.com/2026/09/21/us/northeast-airport-delays-equipment-failure" class="external text">(CNN)</a></li></ul>

However, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latvia" title="Latvia">Latvia</a> later blocks the deal. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.billionaires.africa/2026/09/21/billionaire-alisher-usmanov-is-set-to-come-off-eu-sanctions-after-four-years/" class="external text">(Billionaires.Africa)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.reuters.com/world/europe/latvian-pm-opposes-deal-lift-sanctions-russia-linked-billionaires-usmanov-2026-09-22/" class="external text">(Reuters)</a></li></ul></li></ul>

<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Vermont_shooting_of_Palestinian_students" title="2023 Vermont shooting of Palestinian students">2023 Vermont shooting of Palestinian students</a>
<ul><li>Jason Eaton is found guilty for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Violent_incidents_in_reaction_to_the_Gaza_war" title="Violent incidents in reaction to the Gaza war">shooting</a> and critically wounding three <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestinans" title="Palestinans" class="mw-redirect">Palestinian</a> students in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Burlington,_Vermont" title="Burlington, Vermont">Burlington</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vermont" title="Vermont">Vermont</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States" title="United States">United States</a>, who wore <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestinian_keffiyeh" title="Palestinian keffiyeh">keffiyehs</a> in solidarity with <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Palestine" title="Palestine">Palestine</a> in the wake of the beginning of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaza_war" title="Gaza war">Gaza war</a>. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/sep/21/vermont-man-guilty-attempted-murder-palestinian-students" class="external text">(<i>The Guardian</i>)</a> <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.indystar.com/story/news/crime/2026/09/21/guilty-verdict-jason-eaton-vermont-shooting-palestinian-students-burlington/91877036007/" class="external text">(<i>IndyStar</i>)</a></li></ul></li>
<li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Singapore" title="Singapore">Singapore</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Land_Transport_Authority" title="Land Transport Authority">Land Transport Authority</a> introduces <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fine_City" title="Fine City" class="mw-redirect">fines</a> of up to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S$" title="S$" class="mw-redirect">S$</a>500 (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US$" title="US$" class="mw-redirect">US$</a>390) for eating on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_buses_of_Singapore" title="Public buses of Singapore">public buses</a> or placing feet on seats, while severe cleanliness or safety violations can incur penalties of up to S$5,000 (US$3,900). <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.afp.com/en/propping-feet-bus-seats-carries-400-penalty-fine-city-singapore" class="external text">(AFP)</a></li></ul>

<p><b>Politics and elections</b></p>
<ul><li><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mexico" title="Mexico">Mexico</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Secretariat_of_Public_Education" title="Secretariat of Public Education">Secretariat of Public Education</a> introduces nationwide rules restricting <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mobile_phone_use_in_schools" title="Mobile phone use in schools">cellphone</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/One-to-one_computing" title="One-to-one computing">tablet use</a> throughout the school day in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Education_in_Mexico#Primary_school" title="Education in Mexico">public primary and secondary schools</a>, effective November 3. <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Education_in_Mexico#High_school" title="Education in Mexico">High schools</a> set their own limits for educational use. <a rel="mw:ExtLink nofollow" href="https://www.thestar.com.my/tech/tech-news/2026/09/22/mexico-joins-countries-banning-mobile-phones-in-schools" class="external text">(AFP via <i>The Star</i>)</a></li></ul>
